```python
async def test_the_feed_reads_on_with_a_cursor(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me,
        DATABASE["id"],
        [CanvasModuleItem(number, "3주차", f"강의 {number}", "Page", None) for number in (1, 2, 3)],
        now=now,
    )
    # 처음 본 시각을 달리해 순서를 못 박는다
    for number, minutes in ((1, 0), (2, 5), (3, 10)):
        await conn.execute(
            "update lms_module_items set first_seen_at = %s where canvas_item_id = %s",
            (now + timedelta(minutes=minutes), number),
        )
    since = now - timedelta(days=1)

    first, cursor = await repo.updates(me, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item"], limit=2)
    assert [one.title for one in first] == ["강의 3", "강의 2"]  # 최신 먼저
    assert cursor is not None
    rest, last = await repo.updates(
        me, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item"], limit=2, cursor=cursor
    )
    assert [one.title for one in rest] == ["강의 1"]
    assert last is None


async def test_the_feed_pages_through_rows_seen_at_the_same_moment(
    world: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    """한 동기화에서 들어온 행은 `first_seen_at`이 모두 같다. 그때도 빠지거나 겹치지 않아야 한다."""
    repo, me, now = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me,
        DATABASE["id"],
        [
            CanvasModuleItem(number, "3주차", f"강의 {number}", "Page", None)
            for number in range(1, 6)
        ],
        now=now,
    )
    await repo.save_assignments(
        me,
        course,
        [
            _one(20 + number, title=f"과제 {number}", due=now + timedelta(days=9))
            for number in range(3)
        ],
        now=now,
    )
    since = now - timedelta(days=1)

    seen: list[str] = []
    cursor = None
    for _ in range(10):  # 넉넉히 돌려 본다(8행이면 네 쪽이다)
        page, cursor = await repo.updates(
            me, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item", "assignment"], limit=2, cursor=cursor
        )
        seen += [one.id for one in page]
        if cursor is None:
            break
    assert len(seen) == 8
    assert len(set(seen)) == 8  # 겹치지 않는다


async def test_the_feed_shows_the_file_i_uploaded_for_an_item(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """항목 알림에서 올린 PDF가 있으면 화면이 "요약 보기"를 띄운다(명세 9장)."""
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    saved = await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "3주차", "강의", "Page", None)], now=now
    )
    row = await (
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into files (user_id, kind, origin, storage_path, sha256,"
            " lms_module_item_id) values (%s, 'lecture_material', 'upload', 'p/1', 'abc', %s)"
            " returning id",
            (me, saved.created[0].id),
        )
    ).fetchone()

    items, _ = await repo.updates(me, since=now - timedelta(days=1), now=now, kinds=["module_item"])
    assert items[0].uploaded_file_id == str(row["id"])


async def test_the_feed_is_not_someone_elses(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """항목은 과목 공통이지만, 그 과목을 듣지 않는 사람에게는 보이지 않는다."""
    repo, me, other, now, conn = (
        world["repo"],
        world["me"],
        world["other"],
        world["now"],
        world["conn"],
    )
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "3주차", "강의", "Page", None)], now=now
    )
    since = now - timedelta(days=1)
    mine, _ = await repo.updates(me, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item"])
    assert len(mine) == 1
    theirs, _ = await repo.updates(other, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item"])
    assert theirs == []

    # 남이 올린 파일은 내 피드에 붙지 않는다
    await repo.save_courses(other, [DATABASE], now=now)
    saved_id = mine[0].id
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into files (user_id, kind, origin, storage_path, sha256, lms_module_item_id)"
        " values (%s, 'lecture_material', 'upload', 'p/2', 'def', %s)",
        (other, saved_id),
    )
    again, _ = await repo.updates(me, since=since, now=now, kinds=["module_item"])
    assert again[0].uploaded_file_id is None


async def test_a_dropped_course_leaves_the_feed(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "3주차", "강의", "Page", None)], now=now
    )
    await conn.execute("update lms_courses set is_active = false where user_id = %s", (me,))
    items, _ = await repo.updates(me, since=now - timedelta(days=1), now=now, kinds=["module_item"])
    assert items == []


# ── 과목 상세 (F-14·F-51·F-52, GET /lms/courses/{id}) ──────────────────────

LMS_BASE = "https://lms.test"


async def test_the_course_detail_fills_every_tab(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    await repo.save_assignments(
        me,
        course,
        [
            _one(11, title="나중 과제", due=now + timedelta(days=9)),
            _one(12, title="곧 과제", due=now + timedelta(days=1)),
        ],
        now=now,
    )
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me,
        DATABASE["id"],
        [CanvasModuleItem(1, "3주차", "3주차 강의", "ExternalTool", "https://lms.test/i/1")],
        now=now,
    )
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into lms_announcements (canvas_course_id, canvas_topic_id, title, summary,"
        " category, posted_at) values (%s, 9001, '강의실 변경', '301호에서 해요',"
        " 'schedule_change', %s)",
        (DATABASE["id"], now),
    )
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into lms_announcements (canvas_course_id, canvas_topic_id, title, posted_at,"
        " is_section_specific) values (%s, 9002, '2분반만 보세요', %s, true)",
        (DATABASE["id"], now),
    )
    syllabus = await (
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into syllabi (user_id, lms_course_id, course_name) values (%s, %s, '데이터베이스')"
            " returning id",
            (me, course),
        )
    ).fetchone()
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into syllabus_items (syllabus_id, item_type, title, starts_at, is_confirmed)"
        " values (%s, 'midterm', '중간고사', %s, true)",
        (syllabus["id"], now + timedelta(days=20)),
    )
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into files (user_id, lms_course_id, kind, origin, storage_path, sha256,"
        " original_name) values (%s, %s, 'lecture_material', 'upload', 'p/1', 'abc', '3주차.pdf')",
        (me, course),
    )

    detail = await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=now, lms_base=LMS_BASE)
    assert detail is not None
    assert detail.course.short_name == "데이터베이스"
    assert detail.course.has_syllabus is True
    assert detail.course.html_url == f"{LMS_BASE}/courses/{DATABASE['id']}"
    assert detail.course.portal_syllabus_url is not None
    assert [one.title for one in detail.assignments] == ["곧 과제", "나중 과제"]  # 마감 가까운 순
    assert [one.title for one in detail.module_items] == ["3주차 강의"]
    item = detail.module_items[0]
    assert (item.item_type, item.html_url) == ("ExternalTool", "https://lms.test/i/1")
    assert item.is_new is False  # 첫 수집이라 본 시각이 올라갔다(F-52)
    assert item.uploaded_file_id is None
    assert [one.title for one in detail.announcements] == ["강의실 변경"]  # 분반 한정은 빠진다
    assert detail.announcements[0].category == "schedule_change"
    assert [one.title for one in detail.syllabus_items] == ["중간고사"]
    assert detail.syllabus_items[0].is_confirmed is True
    assert [one.original_name for one in detail.materials] == ["3주차.pdf"]
    assert detail.materials[0].status == "pending"  # 요약 전이면 pending이다


async def test_opening_a_course_marks_it_seen(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """`is_new`는 열기 전 기준이고, 열고 나면 새 자료 수가 0이 된다(명세 9장)."""
    repo, me, now = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "1주차", "01", "Page", None)], now=now
    )
    later = now + timedelta(days=3)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me,
        DATABASE["id"],
        [
            CanvasModuleItem(1, "1주차", "01", "Page", None),
            CanvasModuleItem(2, "2주차", "02", "Page", None),
        ],
        now=later,
    )
    assert (await repo.courses(me, now=later))[0].new_materials == 1

    detail = await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=later, lms_base=LMS_BASE)
    assert detail is not None
    assert {one.title: one.is_new for one in detail.module_items} == {"01": False, "02": True}

    assert (await repo.courses(me, now=later))[0].new_materials == 0  # 봤으니 0이다
    again = await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=later, lms_base=LMS_BASE)
    assert again is not None
    assert [one.is_new for one in again.module_items] == [False, False]


async def test_opening_a_course_does_not_turn_the_clock_back(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """그새 첫 수집이 밀어 둔 본 시각을 지우면, 지난 자료가 다시 "새 자료"가 된다(F-52)."""
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    ahead = now + timedelta(minutes=10)
    await conn.execute("update lms_courses set last_viewed_at = %s where id = %s", (ahead, course))
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "1주차", "01", "Page", None)], now=now
    )

    assert await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=now, lms_base=LMS_BASE) is not None
    row = await (
        await conn.execute("select last_viewed_at from lms_courses where id = %s", (course,))
    ).fetchone()
    assert row["last_viewed_at"] == ahead  # 뒤로 돌아가지 않았다


async def test_the_new_mark_is_read_before_the_course_is_marked_seen(
    world: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    """먼저 본 시각을 올리면 그 응답의 `is_new`가 전부 false가 된다(명세 9장)."""
    repo, me, now = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"]

    later = now + timedelta(days=1)
    await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "1주차", "01 - 소개", "Page", None)], now=later
    )

    detail = await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=later, lms_base=LMS_BASE)
    assert detail is not None
    assert [one.is_new for one in detail.module_items] == [True]  # 이번에 열기 전 기준

async def test_the_detail_does_not_borrow_another_students_rows(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """주차 항목만 과목 공통이다. 과제·계획서·올린 자료는 내 것만 나와야 한다."""
    repo, me, other, now, conn = (
        world["repo"],
        world["me"],
        world["other"],
        world["now"],
        world["conn"],
    )
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    await repo.save_courses(other, [DATABASE], now=now)
    mine = await _course_id(world)
    theirs = str(
        (
            await (
                await conn.execute(
                    "select id from lms_courses where user_id = %s and canvas_course_id = %s",
                    (other, DATABASE["id"]),
                )
            ).fetchone()
        )["id"]
    )
    # 남의 행을 **내 과목 id**에 붙인다. 남의 과목 id에 붙이면 과목 조건만으로 걸러져서
    # user_id 조건이 일하는지 보이지 않는다(검토에서 걸렸다)
    await repo.save_assignments(other, mine, [_one(11, title="남의 과제")], now=now)
    saved = await repo.save_module_items(
        me, DATABASE["id"], [CanvasModuleItem(1, "1주차", "01", "Page", None)], now=now
    )
    syllabus = await (
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into syllabi (user_id, lms_course_id, course_name) values (%s, %s, '남의 계획서')"
            " returning id",
            (other, mine),
        )
    ).fetchone()
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into syllabus_items (syllabus_id, item_type, title) values (%s, 'final', '남의 기말')",
        (syllabus["id"],),
    )
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into files (user_id, lms_course_id, kind, origin, storage_path, sha256,"
        " original_name, lms_module_item_id)"
        " values (%s, %s, 'lecture_material', 'upload', 'p/9', 'xyz', '남의 자료.pdf', %s)",
        (other, mine, saved.created[0].id),
    )

    await repo.save_assignments(other, theirs, [_one(12, title="남의 다른 과제")], now=now)

    detail = await repo.course_detail(me, mine, now=now, lms_base=LMS_BASE)
    assert detail is not None
    assert detail.assignments == []
    assert detail.syllabus_items == []
    assert detail.materials == []
    assert detail.course.has_syllabus is False
    assert [one.title for one in detail.module_items] == ["01"]  # 항목은 공통이라 보인다
    assert detail.module_items[0].uploaded_file_id is None  # 남이 올린 파일은 안 붙는다


async def test_the_detail_shows_my_summary_status(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    repo, me, now, conn = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"], world["conn"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    file_id = (
        await (
            await conn.execute(
                "insert into files (user_id, lms_course_id, kind, origin, storage_path, sha256,"
                " original_name) values (%s, %s, 'lecture_material', 'upload', 'p/2', 'def',"
                " '요약된.pdf') returning id",
                (me, course),
            )
        ).fetchone()
    )["id"]
    await conn.execute(
        "insert into materials (file_id, status, overall_summary) values (%s, 'succeeded', '요약')",
        (file_id,),
    )
    detail = await repo.course_detail(me, course, now=now, lms_base=LMS_BASE)

        ("요약된.pdf", "succeeded")
    ]


# ── 캘린더 제목 (F-51·F-32) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────


async def test_assignment_titles_put_the_course_first(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """캘린더 일정 제목이다. 과목을 앞세워 어느 수업 과제인지 보이게 한다."""
    repo, me, now = world["repo"], world["me"], world["now"]
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    changes = await repo.save_assignments(
        me, course, [_one(11, due=now + timedelta(days=3))], now=now
    )
    assignment_id = changes.created[0].id

    titles = await repo.assignment_titles(me, [assignment_id])
    assert titles == {assignment_id: "[과제] 데이터베이스 · 실습 2주차 결과 제출"}
    assert await repo.assignment_titles(me, []) == {}


async def test_assignment_titles_leave_out_what_is_gone(world: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    repo, me, other, now, conn = (
        world["repo"],
        world["me"],
        world["other"],
        world["now"],
        world["conn"],
    )
    await repo.save_courses(me, [DATABASE], now=now)
    course = await _course_id(world)
    changes = await repo.save_assignments(
        me, course, [_one(11, due=now + timedelta(days=3)), _one(12, title="사라질 과제")], now=now
    )
    kept, gone = (one.id for one in changes.created)
    await repo.save_assignments(me, course, [_one(11, due=now + timedelta(days=3))], now=now)

    titles = await repo.assignment_titles(me, [kept, gone])
    assert set(titles) == {kept}  # 사라진 과제는 빠진다
    assert await repo.assignment_titles(other, [kept]) == {}  # 남의 과제도 아니다

    await conn.execute("update lms_courses set is_active = false where user_id = %s", (me,))
    assert await repo.assignment_titles(me, [kept]) == {}  # 수강을 뺀 과목도 빠진다
```

#### `api/tests/test_lms_api.py`

```python
"""LearningX 연결 API(F-50): 토큰 확인·저장·교체·해제와 과목 목록.
LearningX 호출은 respx로 가로채고 SQL은 test_lms_db.py가 본다."""

from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

import httpx
import pytest
import respx
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.agent_log import using
from app.clock import current_time
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.crypto import decrypt
from app.db import get_log_connect
from app.lms.parse import course_name
from app.main import create_app
from app.repositories.lms import Connection, get_lms_repo, ordered
from app.schemas.lms import CourseItem
from tests.conftest import USER_ID, make_token
from tests.fakes import FakeLogConnection
from tests.lms_samples import COURSES, DATABASE, MINE, USERS_SELF

CONNECTION = "/api/v1/lms/connection"
COURSES_PATH = "/api/v1/lms/courses"
BASE = "https://lms.test"
TOKEN = "18742~pAtB1g3TestTokenValue0000000000"
NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 16, 3, 0, tzinfo=UTC)  # 2026-10-16 12:00 KST


class FakeLmsRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.row: Connection | None = None
        self.saved_courses: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.deactivated = 0

    async def connection(self, user_id: str) -> Connection | None:
        assert user_id == USER_ID
        return self.row

    async def save_connection(
        self, user_id: str, *, token: str, last4: str, canvas_user_id: int | None
    ) -> None:
        assert user_id == USER_ID
        self.row = Connection(
            status="active",
            token_last4=last4,
            access_token=token,
            canvas_user_id=canvas_user_id,
            last_synced_at=None,  # 연결은 과목까지만 받는다(첫 동기화가 받는다)
            last_error=None,
        )

    async def mark_error(self, user_id: str, message: str) -> None:
        if self.row:
            self.row = Connection(**{**self.row.__dict__, "status": "error", "last_error": message})

    async def delete_connection(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if self.row is None:
            return False
        self.row = None
        return True

    async def save_courses(
        self, user_id: str, courses: list[dict[str, Any]], *, now: datetime
    ) -> int:
        assert user_id == USER_ID
        if courses:  # 실제 저장소도 빈 목록으로는 과목을 내리지 않는다
            self.saved_courses = courses
        return len(courses)

    async def courses(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> list[CourseItem]:
        assert user_id == USER_ID
        items = []
        for one in self.saved_courses:
            parsed = course_name(one["name"])
            items.append(
                CourseItem(
                    id=f"course-{one['id']}",
                    canvas_course_id=one["id"],
                    name=one["name"],
                    short_name=parsed.short_name,
                    has_syllabus=False,
                    portal_syllabus_url=parsed.syllabus_url,
                    open_assignments=0,
                    new_materials=0,
                    next_due=None,
                )
            )
        return items


@pytest.fixture
def repo() -> FakeLmsRepository:
    return FakeLmsRepository()


@pytest.fixture
def log() -> FakeLogConnection:
    return FakeLogConnection()


@pytest.fixture
def client(
    settings: Settings, repo: FakeLmsRepository, log: FakeLogConnection
) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    app = create_app(settings)
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: Settings(
        **{**settings.model_dump(), "lms_api_base": BASE}
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_lms_repo] = lambda: repo
    app.dependency_overrides[get_log_connect] = lambda: using(log)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: NOW
    with TestClient(app) as started:
        yield started


def headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {make_token()}"}


def learningx(*, me: int = 200, courses: int = 200) -> None:
    respx.get(f"{BASE}/api/v1/users/self").mock(
        httpx.Response(me, json=USERS_SELF if me == 200 else {"errors": [{"message": "no"}]})
    )
    respx.get(url__startswith=f"{BASE}/api/v1/courses").mock(
        httpx.Response(courses, json=COURSES if courses == 200 else {"errors": []})
    )


@respx.mock
def test_it_connects_and_keeps_only_my_student_courses(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository, log: FakeLogConnection, settings: Settings
) -> None:
    learningx()
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 201
    body = response.json()
    assert body["status"] == "active"
    assert body["token_last4"] == TOKEN[-4:]
    assert body["courses"] == len(MINE)  # 조교 과목과 준비중 과목은 뺀다
    assert body["first_sync_run_id"] == log.run_id
    assert [one["id"] for one in repo.saved_courses] == [one["id"] for one in MINE]
    # 토큰은 암호화해서만 저장하고 응답에는 끝 4자리만 간다
    assert repo.row is not None
    assert repo.row.access_token != TOKEN
    assert decrypt(repo.row.access_token, settings.token_encryption_key) == TOKEN
    assert TOKEN not in response.text
    # 실행과 도구 호출이 기록됐고, 도구 입력에 토큰이 없다
    assert log.wrote("insert into agent_runs")
    assert log.tools() == ["check_lms_token", "fetch_lms_courses"]
    assert all(TOKEN not in str(params) for _, params in log.statements)


@respx.mock
def test_a_wrong_token_is_refused_and_nothing_is_saved(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository, log: FakeLogConnection
) -> None:
    learningx(me=401)
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID"
    assert repo.row is None and repo.saved_courses == []
    # 실패한 토큰 확인도 실행으로 남는다(지표에 쓴다). 과목은 받지 않는다
    assert log.tools() == ["check_lms_token"]
    assert any(
        "status = %s" in query and "failed" in str(params) for query, params in log.statements
    )


@respx.mock
def test_when_the_course_list_fails_the_connection_is_not_saved(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository
) -> None:
    learningx(courses=500)
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 502
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "LMS_API_ERROR"
    assert repo.row is None


@respx.mock
def test_a_token_that_lost_access_while_listing_courses_asks_to_reconnect(
    client: TestClient,
) -> None:
    learningx(courses=401)
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID"


@respx.mock
def test_connecting_again_replaces_the_token(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository, settings: Settings
) -> None:
    learningx()
    client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    second = "18742~sEcOnDtOkEnValue00000000000000"
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": f"  {second}  "}, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 201
    assert response.json()["token_last4"] == second[-4:]  # 앞뒤 공백은 떼고 받는다
    assert repo.row is not None
    assert decrypt(repo.row.access_token, settings.token_encryption_key) == second


@respx.mock
def test_it_shows_the_connection_and_forgets_it_on_disconnect(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository
) -> None:
    assert client.get(CONNECTION, headers=headers()).status_code == 404
    assert client.delete(CONNECTION, headers=headers()).status_code == 404

    learningx()
    client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    view = client.get(CONNECTION, headers=headers())
    assert view.status_code == 200
    assert view.json() == {
        "status": "active",
        "token_last4": TOKEN[-4:],
        "last_synced_at": None,  # 연결 직후다. 과제는 첫 동기화가 받는다
        "last_error": None,
    }
    assert client.delete(CONNECTION, headers=headers()).status_code == 204
    assert client.get(CONNECTION, headers=headers()).status_code == 404
    assert repo.row is None


@respx.mock
def test_the_course_list_has_the_short_name_and_the_portal_link(client: TestClient) -> None:
    learningx()
    client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    items = client.get(COURSES_PATH, headers=headers()).json()["items"]
    assert [one["short_name"] for one in items] == [
        "데이터베이스",
        "기계학습",
        "대학원 세미나(비정규)",
    ]
    assert "suup=23525" in items[0]["portal_syllabus_url"]
    assert items[2]["portal_syllabus_url"] is None  # 과목명 꼴이 달라 링크를 만들 수 없다
    assert items[0]["name"] == "202620HY23525_데이터베이스"  # 원래 이름은 그대로 둔다


@respx.mock
def test_an_empty_course_list_does_not_wipe_the_courses_i_had(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository
) -> None:
    learningx()
    client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert len(repo.saved_courses) == len(MINE)

    respx.get(url__startswith=f"{BASE}/api/v1/courses").mock(httpx.Response(200, json=[]))
    again = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert again.status_code == 201
    assert again.json()["courses"] == 0
    assert len(repo.saved_courses) == len(MINE)  # 앞서 받은 과목이 그대로 남는다


@respx.mock
def test_courses_i_only_assist_or_that_are_not_open_yet_are_left_out(
    client: TestClient, repo: FakeLmsRepository
) -> None:
    learningx()
    no_state = {**DATABASE, "id": 219004}
    no_state.pop("workflow_state")  # 열렸는지 알 수 없는 과목도 세지 않는다
    huge = {**DATABASE, "id": 2**70}  # bigint를 넘는 번호는 저장하지 않는다(DB가 터진다)
    respx.get(url__startswith=f"{BASE}/api/v1/courses").mock(
        httpx.Response(200, json=[*COURSES, no_state, huge])
    )
    response = client.post(CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN}, headers=headers())
    assert response.json()["courses"] == len(MINE)
    assert [one["id"] for one in repo.saved_courses] == [one["id"] for one in MINE]


def test_the_course_list_order_does_not_depend_on_the_database() -> None:
    """정렬은 ordered()가 한다. DB의 정렬 규칙(collation)은 환경마다 한글·숫자 앞뒤가 바뀐다.

    DB 없이 도는 테스트라 CI에서만 걸리는 일이 없다(2026-10-16에 그렇게 한 번 걸렸다).
    """
    names = [
        "202620HY23534_기계학습",
        "대학원 세미나(비정규)",
        "2026 겨울 특강",
        "202620HY25123_AI+X공학융합프로젝트",
        "202620HY23525_데이터베이스",
    ]
    items = [
        CourseItem(
            id=f"course-{index}",
            canvas_course_id=index,
            name=name,
            short_name=course_name(name).short_name,
            has_syllabus=False,
            portal_syllabus_url=None,
            open_assignments=0,
            new_materials=0,
            next_due=None,
        )
        for index, name in enumerate(names, start=1)
    ]
    assert [one.short_name for one in ordered(items)] == [
        "2026 겨울 특강",  # 과목명 꼴이 아니라 이름 전체가 화면 이름이다
        "AI+X공학융합프로젝트",
        "기계학습",
        "대학원 세미나(비정규)",
        "데이터베이스",
    ]
    # 어느 순서로 들어와도 결과가 같다
    assert [one.name for one in ordered(items[::-1])] == [one.name for one in ordered(items)]


def test_it_refuses_a_body_with_another_field(client: TestClient) -> None:
    response = client.post(
        CONNECTION, json={"access_token": TOKEN, "lms": "other"}, headers=headers()
    )
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "VALIDATION_FAILED"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("method", "path"),
    [
        ("post", CONNECTION),
        ("get", CONNECTION),
        ("delete", CONNECTION),
        ("get", COURSES_PATH),
        ("get", f"{COURSES_PATH}/11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111"),
        ("post", "/api/v1/lms/sync"),
        ("get", "/api/v1/lms/assignments"),
        ("get", "/api/v1/lms/updates"),
    ],
)
def test_every_lms_path_needs_a_login(client: TestClient, method: str, path: str) -> None:
    body = {"json": {"access_token": TOKEN}} if path == CONNECTION and method == "post" else {}
    assert getattr(client, method)(path, **body).status_code == 401
```
